- **char-anim**: Makes character and monster animation that feels alive and in character, from rig check to key-pose blocking, keyframe in-betweening, Mixamo base clips for humanoid heroes, render-and-fix loops, defect and quality gates, and game-ready glb export. ([twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills](https://github.com/twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/char-anim))
- **char-art-system**: 캐릭터 아트를 생성 모델(로컬 ComfyUI)로 만들고 정체성을 유지한 채 변형·시트로 확장한다. 아트 3축(UI·이펙트·캐릭터) 중 유일하게 절차적 생성이 안 되는 축이라 백엔드 세팅이 선행된다. 생성 -&gt; 배경 제거(알파) -&gt; 규격 통일 -&gt; 아틀라스까지 커스텀 노드 없이 잇는다. "캐릭터 만들어줘", "캐릭터 시안", "표정/포즈 변형", "캐릭터 LoRA", "ComfyUI로 뽑아줘", "정체성 유지", "스프라이트 만들어줘", "배경 투명하게", "아틀라스로 묶어줘" 같은 요청에 사용. 소유: artist + developer. 주의: 백엔드(ComfyUI)가 안 떠 있으면 판정은 실패가 아니라 **측정 불가**다 — references/comfyui-setup.md 부터. - **character-identity-asset-studio**: 基于角色简报、人脸参考和服装参考，建立可复用的角色身份与资产包；包含人脸确认、四张候选生成、造型确认，以及正面、背面和人脸锚点交付。 ([qxryz/workflowgenerator](https://github.com/qxryz/workflowgenerator/tree/HEAD/skills/library/character-identity-asset-studio) · ★47)
- **character-identity-bible**: Use when the task is to create a durable character identity specification from approved references. ([voyager67/Agentic-Skill-Garage](https://github.com/voyager67/Agentic-Skill-Garage/tree/HEAD/skills/character-identity-bible) · ★3)
- **character-identity-lock-setup**: Builds a locked visual-identity reference system for a recurring character — a set of reference images (a multi-panel turnaround sheet plus a standalone bust portrait) for every distinct age or life-stage that character appears at, generated from real photo identity sources and hosted so every later generation can attach the correct one. Prevents a character's face from drifting between shots, and prevents an AI image generator from ever inventing the face freehand from text alone. Use at the start of any project with a recurring character who appears across multiple ages, outfits, or a long… ([teen-boop/AiProductionPipeline](https://github.com/teen-boop/AiProductionPipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/character-identity-lock-setup) · ★6)
- **character-identity-narrative-modeler**: Use when a character's self-story, public mask, social roles, selected memories, life chapters, identity audiences, assigned identity, possible selves, or response to identity threat is a load-bearing story problem. ([yeisme/auctra-novel-skills](https://github.com/yeisme/auctra-novel-skills/tree/HEAD/character-modeling/character-identity-narrative-modeler) · ★1)
- **character-illustration**: 手動呼び出し専用（Claude Code では /character-illustration、Codex では $character-illustration）。 rpg-character-profile で作ったキャラ設定と「イラストの構図」から、背景付きのキャラクターイラストの依頼文を作り、 キャラ設定の全文を添えて Codex CLI の画像生成に依頼し、生成されたイラストを見せる。 イラストやキャラクターの話題というだけでは自動で使わない。 ([yn1323/baryonyx](https://github.com/yn1323/baryonyx/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/character-illustration))
- **character-illustration-batch**: 教練說任何圖片生成指令（「跑圖」「生圖」「漫畫」「分鏡」「storyboard」「章回小說」「插畫集」「用我照片當 reference」「角色要保持一致」「場景圖」⋯）→ 一律預設走 LK888（2026-09-28 教練親定，不再問 provider / model）。鎖臉版走 `/v1/images/edits` + 教練 reference；教練說「敘事版 / 不鎖臉 / 以劇情為主 / 避免視覺疲勞」→ 走 Narrative 版（`/v1/images/generations` 純文字，不帶 reference）。跑前必先讓教練選通知節奏（A 每張推 / B 每 N 張聚合 / C 跑完一次），預設 A。 ([Coach1973/dashu-coach-ai-lessons](https://github.com/Coach1973/dashu-coach-ai-lessons/tree/HEAD/ops/xiaolongxia_workshop_skills/main/character-illustration-batch))
- **character-image**: The prompt for a character's one vertical reference image, which the video model redraws the character from in every scene; lines without a value are left out. ([phongtran231/video-skills](https://github.com/phongtran231/video-skills/tree/HEAD/images/character-image))
- **character-image-collection**: 一句话指定角色与主题，调用本地 ComfyUI 生成成组角色图，再制作缩放模糊展示或纯交叉溶解的竖屏图像合集视频；支持已有图片与局部重做。 ([Shenrui-Ma/video-regen-recipes](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma/video-regen-recipes/tree/HEAD/templates/character-showcase/image-collection) · ★9)
- **character-image-design**: 设计任意原创角色的人设图、立绘、角色设定图、三视图及主题插画；从文字或参考图提取身份特征，在换画风、服装和动作时维持角色一致性。用于视觉角色创作，不用于纯文字机器人提示词、QQ 上架或游戏控件实现；明确鲸鱼娘时优先专用技能。 ([ByteWanderer-hub/xingyuan-ai-hub](https://github.com/ByteWanderer-hub/xingyuan-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/character-image-design) · ★1)
